## 🌟 About the Collection

> A comprehensive archive of milestone Bangladeshi mixed rock albums, underground compilations, and milestone tributes in pristine lossless FLAC and WAV audio with full packaging artwork scans. Featuring seminal releases including the **Agontuk** series, **Shopnochura** series, **Rock** series, **Din Bodol**, **Live Now**, **Onushilon**, **Neon Aloy Shagotom**, **Bonno**, **Shomorpon**, and the monumental 64-band quadruple compilation **Hatiar (2012)**.

## 1. Onushilon (অনুশীলন) (2002)
- **Album:** Onushilon (অনুশীলন)
- **Release Year:** 2002
- **Record Label:** G-Series
- **Audio Quality:** Lossless FLAC (16-bit / 44.1 kHz)

<p align="center">
  <img src="Onushilon%20%282002%29/Album%20Cover/Onushilon%20%5BFront-Back%5D.jpg" width="300" alt="Onushilon (2002) Cover" />
</p>

### 📖 About the Album
A historic underground compilation album released in 2002 under G-Series featuring groundbreaking tracks from Aurthohin, Durey, Faith, Artcell, Metal Maze, Black, and Cryptic Fate. This landmark release captured the very beginning of Dhaka's alternative rock and progressive metal renaissance.

## 2. Agontuk 1 (আগন্তুক ১) (2003)
- **Album:** Agontuk 1 (আগন্তুক ১)
- **Release Year:** 2003
- **Record Label:** Soundtek
- **Audio Quality:** Lossless FLAC (16-bit / 44.1 kHz)

<p align="center">
  <img src="Agontuk%201%20%282003%29/Album%20Cover/Agontuk%201%20%5BFront%5D.jpg" width="300" alt="Agontuk 1 (2003) Cover" />
</p>

### 📖 About the Album
The legendary first installment of the Agontuk mixed album series produced by Iqbal Asif Jewel and released under Soundtek in 2003. Featuring iconic tracks from Aurthohin, Black, Artcell, Vibe, Cryptic Fate, and Poizon Green, Agontuk 1 ignited the nationwide youth rock revolution.

The historic debut installment of the milestone *Shopnochura* ("Dream Peak") series coordinated by Tony (Black) and released under G-Series in 2004. ### 📖 About the Album
The celebrated third edition of the *Shopnochura* series, released in 2007 under G-Series. Showcasing 13 seminal alternative tracks including Black's *"E Karonei (এ কারণেই)"*, Yaatri's *"Jonmechi Tai (জন্মেছি তাই)"*, and Shironamhin's iconic *"Train (ট্রেন)"*.

### 📖 About the Album
A landmark 2011 tribute compilation album dedicated to mystic bards **Lalon Shah** and **Hason Raja**, featuring modern progressive rock, hard rock, and fusion arrangements performed by heavyweight bands **Warfaze**, **Habib Wahid**, and **Aurthohin**.

## 20. Hatiar (হাতিয়ার) (2012)
- **Album:** Hatiar (হাতিয়ার) — *The Largest Band Mixed Album in History*
- **Release Year:** 2012
- **Coordinator:** Duray
- **Record Label:** G-Series Exclusive
- **Format:** 4-Disc Compilation (64 Bands | 64 Songs)
- **Audio Quality:** Lossless WAV (16-bit / 44.1 kHz)

<p align="center">
  <img src="Hatiar%20%282012%29/Album%20Cover/Hatiar%20%5BFront%5D.jpg" width="300" alt="Hatiar (2012) Cover" />
</p>

### 📖 About the Album
Released in October 2012 under G-Series and coordinated by Duray, *Hatiar* (*"The Largest Band Mixed Album in History"*) is a monumental 4-disc, 64-song compilation uniting 64 bands across the Bangladeshi rock and heavy metal landscape. Featuring powerhouse tracks from Cryptic Fate, Black, Icons, Minerva, Karnival, Sent Men Revolt, Yaatri, Scarecrow, Shohortoli, Ashes, De-Illumination, Poizon Green, and dozens more, *Hatiar* stands as the most expansive multi-disc band compilation ever produced in Bangladesh.

## 21. Shopnochura 4 (স্বপ্নচূড়া ৪) (2014)
- **Album:** Shopnochura 4 (স্বপ্নচূড়া ৪)

- **Audio Quality:** Lossless FLAC (16-bit / 44.1 kHz)

### 📖 About the Album
Released in 2014 under G-Series and coordinated by Tony, *Shopnochura 4* marked the grand return of the franchise, presenting 11 tracks from veteran alternative bands and fresh talents, led by Black's emotive masterpiece *"Kar Jonne"*, Yaatri's *"Ora Bodle Geche"*, and Project Neon's *"Olosh Poka"*.

### 📖 About the Album
A fierce 14-track metal collection released in 2015 by Incursion Music, opening with Artcell's thunderous rendition of Kazi Nazrul Islam's rebellion anthem *Karar Oi Louho Kopat*, followed by high-voltage metal attacks from Bay of Bengal, Powersurge (*Terror Scream*), Scarecrow, Overlord, and Thrash.

### 📦 Git LFS & Downloading Audio

All audio tracks in this repository are managed via **Git LFS** (Large File Storage).

- **One-Tap Direct Download:** Click either the track title or the green `Download FLAC` button next to any song to instantly save the file to your device.
- **To clone the complete discography locally with all audio files:**
  ```bash
  git lfs install
  git clone https://github.com/GhostDog45/Mixed-Albums.git
  ```

### 🙏 Special Thanks & Gratitude
Heartfelt gratitude to **Shohail Ibne Mahbub (XTR)** for his monumental passion and dedication in keeping Bangladeshi band music alive. Through his archival project [**Bangla CD Covers**](https://banglacdcovers.blogspot.com/), he has painstakingly collected, preserved, and scanned original physical CDs and cassette tapes, ensuring that the legacy, artwork, and history of Bangladesh's rock and band movement remain preserved for generations of music lovers.
